## Surprises & Discoveries

_Log anything the audit got wrong, anything that turned out harder/easier than
the finding implied, and any new issue found while fixing._

- **M1 (2026-06-22):** INT-001's size-relative floor `min(size*0.15,48)` flips an
  existing test (`m3-gestures.test.tsx`: `tkShouldCommit(30,0.9,200)` was `true`,
  now `false` — the old test encoded the buggy velocity-only commit). Updated in
  lockstep.
- INT-006 one-frame flash is **not observable in jsdom** (no paint): the behaviour
  test only proves the final z; a source-check guards the `useIsomorphicLayoutEffect`
  mechanism against a regression back to a post-paint `useEffect`.
- Reviewer caught a gap the first pass missed: INT-002 was fixed for the
  move-after-disable path but **not the finger-lift-after-disable** path
  (`onPointerUp` still committed). Now gated on `enabledRef` in both.
- Consumer (acting-as-user) review upgraded INT-008 from "flag only" to carrying
  `lastDelta`/`lastVelocity` (real iOS `pointercancel` commit-despite-steal need),
  and flagged that an `[active]`-only swipe-guard dep would miss a late-mounting
  WebApp — fixed with `[active, apiReady]`.

## Decision Log

_Record API/breaking-change decisions (e.g. role semantics for InlineButtons,
token migrations, shared-hook extractions) with date + rationale._

### 2026-06-22 — All HIGH findings closed (M9-A…I) + refuted set resolved
- **Every `🟠 HIGH` finding is now ticked.** M9-A…F closed all `D-A11Y`/`D-SEC` HIGH; M9-G/H/I closed the remaining real HIGH (FRM-001, INP-003, TYP-003, NAV2-004, SVC-002, SVC-003, LST-001). New locale keys added across these: `online`, `offline`, `unavailable`, `tabs`, `phoneNumber`, `noResults`. New `.tk-sr-only`/`.tk-sr-only-focusable` + `.tk-otp-caret` CSS in tokens.css. Suite at **1006 unit tests green, `tsc` clean**.
- **Refuted / won't-fix HIGH (ticked as consciously addressed, not implemented):**
  - `TCRD-001` (StatTile empty-bars → -Infinity%) — **already covered by TCRD-002**: `max = peak > 0 ? peak : 1` floors the divisor so empty/all-zero bars render 0%, never -Infinity%.
  - `LST-005` (VirtualList remounts rows by index on window shift) — **premise false**: rows are keyed by ABSOLUTE item index (stable per item), so a window shift reuses rows via React keys; no remount. `getKey` is available for identity-based keys.
  - `LST-002` (TKCell `forwardRef(Impl as never)` erases prop types) — **won't-fix, documented ceiling**: the cast is forced by polymorphic-forwardRef variance (a required `title` triggers a variance error without it); the PUBLIC `TKCellProps` type is sound, only the internal impl cast is loose. Marked with a `// ponytail:` note in code.
  - `TYP-002` (`large` text level type-vs-token mismatch) — **refuted**: the value resolves and renders correctly at runtime (the finding itself concedes "it resolves"); the type nuance is cosmetic, no user-visible defect.
  - `FRM-010` (no regression net for phone/mask state-caret-locale logic) — **won't-fix, redundant**: the masked/phone paths are already covered by `m4-field-contract`, `coverage-components`, and the new FRM-001/FRM-002 tests; a dedicated net adds no coverage.

- **2026-06-22 · M1 commit policy.** `tkShouldCommit` gains a 4th `TKCommitPolicy`
  arg (INT-DX-004); default `minDistance = min(size*0.15, 48)` (size-relative, not
  the plan's illustrative flat `24`) so the floor scales 40px-cell↔600px-sheet and
  satisfies the INT-001 boundary cases. Back-compat: existing 3-arg callers
  unchanged.
- **2026-06-22 · M1 shared state.** Overlay z-stack / scroll-lock / swipe-guard
  counters moved from module `let`s onto a `Symbol.for("tg-mini-app-uikit/internal-registry")`
  globalThis slot (INT-005) so duplicate bundle copies share one source of truth;
  hard floor at 0 + dev `console.error` on release-at-zero; `__tkResetSharedState()`
  exported (internal-only) as the test seam.
- **2026-06-22 · M1 controllable.** `useControllable` warns (dev, `console.error`,
  React-parity) on a controlled↔uncontrolled flip and gains a self-documenting
  options-object overload with `name` (INT-DX-007). `defaultValue` stays
  initial-only by design.
- **2026-06-22 · M1 z typing.** `tkZ` is now `Record<TKZLayer,string>` (no
  `as unknown as`); numeric layering math uses the new `tkZIndex(layer, offset)`
  (INT-010/INT-DX-006).
- **2026-06-22 · M1 sequencing of breaking DX.** INT-DX-002/005 (`useDragGesture`
  → `{bind,style}` return) and INT-DX-001 `useModalOverlay` / INT-DX-003
  `useRovingFocus` ship **with their first consumers** (M7 / M3) rather than as
  dead exports in M1; INT-003's actual safety fix (nav `touch-action: pan-y`)
  landed now directly.
- **2026-06-22 · M2 behavior changes (CHANGELOG-worthy for consumers).**
  (1) `TKProvider telegram` mirrors `--tg-theme-*` **scoped to its root by default**
  (was global `<html>`); pass `telegram="global"` for page-level vars. (2)
  `reduceMotion` defaults to `'auto'` — every provider now honors OS reduced-motion
  (`data-tk-motion="off"`); opt out with `reduceMotion={false}`; use the exported
  `useReducedMotion()` to drive your own animations. (3) `motionSpeed` is clamped to
  ≥0.05 (use `reduceMotion` for motion-off, not `motionSpeed={0}`). (4) CloudStorage
  `get/getMany/remove/removeMany` now **reject invalid keys** (the localStorage
  fallback previously returned null). (5) link helpers reject non-allowlisted
  schemes; `openTelegramLink` is tg:/t.me-only.
- **2026-06-22 · M2 FND-DX-005 scope.** `useTelegramEnvironment` returns
  `reason: 'webapp' | 'browser'` — the planned `'mock'` value was dropped because a
  mock WebApp is not soundly distinguishable from a real one at runtime.
- **2026-06-22 · M2 FND-001 contract.** `shareToStory` resolves `true`
  optimistically after a 1500ms timeout when no story event fires (was: hang
  forever); a later event cannot retroactively flip a settled result.
- **2026-06-22 · M3 group semantics + a11y (CHANGELOG-worthy).** Single-select
  `TKInlineButtons` and `TKSegmented` and interactive `TKRating` dropped
  `aria-pressed` for `radiogroup`/`radio` + `aria-checked` (InlineButtons was
  briefly `tablist` but a tablist with no tabpanel fails axe, so it is a
  radiogroup like Segmented); selection follows focus on arrow keys. Pass
  `ariaLabel` for an accessible group name (dev-warns if omitted). Consumers
  styling/testing `[aria-pressed]` on these must migrate to
  `[aria-checked]`/`[aria-selected]`.
- **2026-06-22 · M3 touch targets.** ≥44px applied via an invisible hit-slop
  (negative margins) where visual size must stay small (IconButton, input
  clear/reveal, toast action, chip-remove, interactive Rating stars), and via
  real growth where 44px is appropriate (Segmented, InlineButtons, CardChip).
  `TKTappable` defaults `minTarget=44` (it has no internal consumers); pass
  `minTarget={false}` for an inline text link. Readonly `TKRating` keeps no
  touch target (display-only).
- **2026-06-22 · M3 Rating keyboard.** Arrows ±1, Shift+Arrow ±0.5 (when
  `allowHalf`), Home/End to bounds; value-driven (never clientX). `aria-checked`
  rounds a fractional value up to the nearest star (single-radio compromise).
- **2026-06-22 · M4 blank-by-default (CHANGELOG-worthy).** Uncontrolled `TKSelect`
  and `TKRadioGroup` no longer auto-select the first option (INP-001/CTL-002) —
  they start blank so the placeholder shows and parent state matches the UI; pass
  `defaultValue` to pre-select. Deliberately NO runtime warn (blank is the desired
  default — a warn would punish correct usage). Note the intentional asymmetry:
  `TKSegmented`/tabs keep a first-selected default (a segmented control always has
  one active choice), while select/radio allow "nothing selected".
- **2026-06-22 · M4 SlotPicker reconcile.** `onSlotChange` may now fire
  programmatically with `""` when the selection is reconciled away (slot left
  `slots` or entered `busy`); consumers must not treat every call as a user tap
  (documented on the prop).
- **2026-06-22 · M5 behavior changes (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1) `TKVirtualList.height`
  is now optional, defaulting to `100%` — the parent must have a resolved height or
  the list collapses to 0. (2) `TKTabbar` keys by `tab.id ?? index` (was `tab.label`)
  so duplicate labels render distinct tabs; pass `id` if you reorder tabs. (3)
  `TKCounter` coerces numeric strings, clamps negatives to 0, and applies `max+`
  (a non-numeric ReactNode renders as-is). (4) `TKSteps` clamps `current`; an
  all-complete `current >= length` renders the last step active (no separate
  "all done, none active" state). (5) `TKStatTile`/`TKBars` divisor floors at 1
  only when the peak is ≤0, so fractional (0–1) series keep their scale.

- **2026-06-22 · M7-A composite correctness (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1) `TKSwipeCell`
  default `fullSwipe` flipped `true`→`false`; a full swipe no longer auto-fires the
  first action unless opted in, and even then a destructive first action (`tone:"red"`
  or the new `destructive:true`) opens the row for a confirming tap instead of firing
  (GES-007/CC-01). (2) `TKNavStack` moves focus into the newly-active panel on
  navigation (`focus({preventScroll:true})`, and only when the consumer hasn't already
  focused something inside it). The active panel is a `role="region"` landmark named by
  the new `TKNavPanel label` prop (falls back to `id`); that focus move IS the
  screen-change announcement, so there is no separate live-region (avoids the
  double-announce + machine-id readout the first cut introduced). (3) `TKOnboardingTooltip`
  with an unmeasurable (null) target now renders the step as a centred card over a
  transparent interceptor — never an opaque full-screen blackout (ONB-004) and never a
  dead screen with no Skip/Next. (4) `TKGallery` exposes a localized
  `locale.slidePosition` ("Slide N of M") live region (CRS-005).
- **2026-06-22 · M7-B overlay family (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1) `TKPopper role="dialog"`
  is now a focus-trapping modal (focus moves in, Tab is trapped, Escape closes and
  restores focus to the anchor). It does NOT scrim or inert the background — an
  anchored popover keeps click-outside-to-close and a reachable anchor. Consumers who
  used `role="dialog"` for mere semantics get the trap now; use `tooltip`/`menu` for a
  non-trapping surface. (2) Overlay close now unmounts on the real CSS exit
  (`animationend` + measured duration), so the documented `motionSpeed` knob no longer
  clips the dismiss. (3) `TKDialog` gained `actionsLayout` (`"row"|"stacked"|"auto"`,
  default `auto`): **auto stacks when there are more than two actions**, so existing
  3-button dialogs that were equal-columns now stack (count is of flat children —
  don't wrap actions in one container if you want auto). (4) Modal overlays
  (`TKDialog`/`TKSheet`/`TKActionSheet`) now mark the background `inert` + `aria-hidden`
  so AT can't wander behind the scrim; scrims, sibling overlays, and the toast stack
  are exempt. A consumer live region that must stay reachable over a modal can opt out
  with `data-tk-keep-live`. (5) `TKToastProvider` now portals its stack to the nearest
  `.tk` root (or a `data-tk-portal-root`, which `TKFrame` sets) so a transformed
  ancestor can't re-anchor toasts; it expects a `.tk` ancestor for tokens. (6)
  `TKActionSheet` default accessible name is `locale.actions` (was the literal
  "Actions"). (7) `TKSheet` dev-warns on non-ascending / out-of-range `snapPoints`.
- **2026-06-22 · M7-C carousel/nav/onboarding (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1) `TKGallery`
  snap math now uses `clientWidth + gap` as the per-slide stride (was clientWidth
  only), so multi-slide carousels with a gap center correctly and `onPageChange`
  reports the right index; it also re-centers on resize and its programmatic-scroll
  guard self-clears so a missed/interrupted animation can't permanently swallow
  `onPageChange`. (2) `TKNavStack` dedups a rapid duplicate push of the same
  panel+params within ~300ms (laggy-WebView double-tap); params are compared by JSON,
  key order isn't normalized (a reordered-keys object is a harmless dedup miss), and
  non-serializable params skip dedup entirely so a legitimate push is never swallowed.
  (3) `TKOnboardingTooltip` gained `onSkip` and `dismissable` (default true:
  Escape/scrim/outside-tap end the tour) and is now a `role="dialog"` focus-trap by
  default — pass `trapFocus={false}` for a non-trapping coach-mark that points at an
  interactive target. Empty/shrinking `steps` no longer vanish or mis-fire: the index
  clamps and an empty set calls `onFinish` once.
- **2026-06-22 · M7-D long-press/safe-area (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1) `useLongPress`
  now returns `onClickCapture` and `onKeyDown` in addition to the pointer handlers —
  spread the WHOLE result LAST (`<el {...useLongPress(fn)} />`) and don't re-declare
  those handlers after the spread. It self-suppresses the post-hold synthetic click
  (consumers can drop their manual suppress refs), ignores non-primary pointers and
  right/middle mouse buttons, and a second finger aborts an in-flight hold. Its
  keyboard trigger (ContextMenu/Shift+F10) is a DESKTOP fallback only — long-press
  remains an enhancement and consumers must expose the action via a visible
  AT-reachable control for mobile SR/switch.
  (2) The layout family (`tkSafePad`,
  `TKPage`, `TKBottomBar`, `TKSafeArea`) now reads the overridable `--tk-safe-{edge}`
  token (the same source TKHeader/TKTabbar use), so a provider/consumer override
  applies uniformly; it floors at the JS-measured inset, so an override can grow but
  not shrink below a real device cutout. `TKBottomBar` and `TKPage` content now also
  pad the left/right safe-area insets so a landscape side-notch can't clip actions.
- **2026-06-22 · M8-A/B DX surface (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1) Haptics consistency
  (CC-16): `TKGallery` (`haptics` prop, default true) buzzes `selection` on a landed
  page change; `useLongPress` (`haptic` option, default true) buzzes `impact("medium")`
  on a fired press — both no-op without a `haptics`-enabled `TKTelegramProvider`/`TKApp`.
  (2) New `<TKApp>` batteries-included root (FND-DX-001) composes the Telegram + theme
  + locale (+ toast) providers and auto-syncs the visual theme from the live Telegram
  scheme; one element replaces the manual provider ladder. The individual providers
  stay exported; `TKApp` expects an already-resolved `webApp` (it doesn't strip a
  browser stub).
- **2026-06-22 · M7-E internal DX (breaking for internal callers only — useDragGesture
  is not part of the public package surface).** (1) `useDragGesture` returns
  `{ bind, style }` instead of a bare handler bag. Spread `bind()` for the pointer
  handlers (pass consumer handlers to merge them, user-first, with a `preventDefault`
  veto that gates `pointerdown`) and `style` for the axis-correct `touch-action`
  (`pan-y` for axis x, `pan-x` for axis y); override `touchAction` for a full-claim
  surface. (2) `useModalOverlay({ mounted, active, ref, onClose, ... })` sequences the
  five modal hooks (layer → scroll-lock → swipe-guard → focus-trap → Back) in one
  ordered call and returns `{ scrimZ, panelZ, panelProps, scrimProps }` — `panelProps`
  pre-builds role/`aria-modal`/label-desc/`tabIndex`/zIndex. A node carrying
  `data-tk-keep-live` opts out of the modal's background-inert. The five primitives
  remain exported for bespoke overlays.
- **2026-06-22 · M8 D–J escape hatch + typing + data API (CHANGELOG-worthy).** (1)
  CC-13 repo-wide: every display/feedback/card/carousel/template-card component now
  `forwardRef`s, accepts `className` (merged with built-in classes) and spreads native
  DOM props (`title`/`data-*`/handlers) onto its root — attach a ref, a class, an
  analytics `data-attr` or a tooltip without a wrapper. (2) CC-12: the polymorphic
  atoms (`TKButton`/`TKTappable`/`TKCardCell`) now expose a generic ref — `<TKTappable
  as="a" ref={…}>` yields an `HTMLAnchorElement` ref (was forced to `HTMLElement`). (3)
  `TKGallery` gained an `items` + `renderItem` (+ `getKey`) data API alongside
  `children`; `getKey` keeps slide identity stable on reorder (CRS-DX-006/CRS-008).
